  var shipRate = ''; var shipNote = ''; var shipNoteSuffix = '+';var currentprice = 0;var isLoaded=0;
  function displayMessage(shipRate,shipNote){ if(shipRate == '' && shipNote == ''){$('.promo_banner').html('');$('.product-shipping-note').html('');}else{if(shipRate == -1){$('.promo_banner').html(shipNote);}else if(shipRate == 0){$('.promo_banner').html(shipNote);}else{$('.promo_banner').html(shipNote+' $'+shipRate+shipNoteSuffix);}productShippingNote(shipRate,currentprice);} }
  function productShippingNote(shipRate,currentprice){if(shipRate >= 0){if(shipRate >=  currentprice){var shipprice = Shopify.formatMoney(shipRate*100, "<span class=money>${{amount}}</span>");$('.product-shipping-note').html(shipNote+' <span class="money">'+shipprice.replace(".00","")+'</span>'+shipNoteSuffix);}else{$('.product-shipping-note').html('Qualifies for Free Shipping');}}else{$('.product-shipping-note').html('Lightning-Fast Shipping');} }
  function shippingMessage(shipRate,totalprice,div){if(shipRate >= 0){if (totalprice >= shipRate) {$(div).html("Your order qualifies for FREE SHIPPING!");} else {var away = shipRate - totalprice;$(div).html("You're <span class='money'>"+Shopify.formatMoney( away.toFixed(2), "<span class=money>${{amount}}</span>") +"</span> away from FREE SHIPPING!");}}else{$(div).html('Lightning-Fast Shipping');} }
  function initShipping(country_code){
          if(country_code !=""){  
                  if(country_code == 'US'){ 
                    $('#coin-container').parent().hide();
                    $('.mobile_only.coin_footer').hide();
                    $('body').addClass('freeShipNote'); 
                    shipRate = 80;
                    shipNote = 'Free Shipping On Orders';
                    displayMessage(shipRate,shipNote); 
                  }else if(country_code == 'CA'){
                    $('body').addClass('freeShipNote'); 
                    shipRate = 120;
                    shipNote = 'Free Shipping On Orders';
                    displayMessage(shipRate,shipNote); 
                  }else{
                    $('body').addClass('freeShipNote');
                    displayMessage(-1,'Lightning-Fast Shipping'); 
                  }
                }else{
                  shipRate = 80;
                  shipNote = 'Free Shipping On Orders';
                  displayMessage(-1,'Lightning-Fast Shipping'); 
                }
                $.post('/cart',function( data ) {
                  var totaldoller = (data['total_price'] / 100);
                  if ( shipRate != '' && (country_code == 'US' || country_code == 'CA')){
                    shippingMessage(shipRate,totaldoller,'.shippingmessage');
                  } else {
                    $(".shippingmessage").html("Lightning-Fast Shipping");} 
                }, "json");}

jQuery(document).ready(function() {
  if (window.innerWidth <600) {
    var autocompleteObserver;
    var autocompleteContainer;
    var addCustomHTML = function() {
        autocompleteObserver.disconnect();

      if ($('.snize-ac-results:visible').length) {
            $("html, body").animate({ scrollTop: 0 }, 0);
        }

        autocompleteObserver.observe(autocompleteContainer, {
            childList: true,
            subtree: true
        });
    };

    var waitForEl = function(selector, callback) {
        if (jQuery(selector).length) {
            callback();
        } else {
            setTimeout(function() {
                waitForEl(selector, callback);
            }, 100);
        }
    };

    waitForEl('.snize-ac-results', function() {
        autocompleteContainer = document.querySelector('.snize-ac-results');

        if (autocompleteContainer) {
            $("html, body").animate({ scrollTop: 0 }, 0);
            autocompleteObserver = new MutationObserver(addCustomHTML);
            autocompleteObserver.observe(autocompleteContainer, {
                childList: true,
                subtree: true
            });

            jQuery(window).resize(function() {
                setTimeout(function() {
                    autocompleteObserver.disconnect();
                    addCustomHTML();
                    autocompleteObserver.observe(autocompleteContainer, {
                        childList: true,
                        subtree: true
                    });
                });
            });
        }
    });
  }
});</script>

<script data-cfasync="false" type="text/javascript">
                    var SswInstagramGallery = function ($container, gallery_id, type, product_id, item_selector, min_width, min_distance, columnsCount, min_columns_count) {
            var self = this;
            var item_width = min_width;
            var distance_column = min_distance;
            var container_width = $container.width();
            var column_count = 1;
            var minColumnsCount = min_columns_count || 1;
            var scrolling = false;
            this.initVars = function () {
              var zoom_percent = 0;
              column_count = 1;
              if (container_width <= min_width) {
                item_width = container_width;
                distance_column = min_distance;
              } else {
                for (var i = columnsCount; i >= minColumnsCount; i--) {
                  if (min_columns_count > 1 && i === minColumnsCount) {
                    zoom_percent = 0;
                    distance_column = min_distance;
                    column_count = minColumnsCount;
                    item_width = (container_width - distance_column * (column_count - 1)) / column_count;
                    if (column_count > 1) {
                      distance_column = (container_width - item_width * column_count) / column_count;
                      distance_column = parseFloat(distance_column.toFixed(2));
                    }
                    break;
                  } else {
                    var etalon_container_width = min_width * i + ((i - 1) * min_distance);
                    if (etalon_container_width <= container_width) {
                      zoom_percent = container_width * 100 / etalon_container_width - 100;
                      distance_column = min_distance + (min_distance * zoom_percent / 100);
                      column_count = columnsCount;
                      item_width = (container_width - distance_column * (column_count - 1)) / column_count;
                      if (column_count > 1) {
                        distance_column = (container_width - item_width * column_count) / (column_count - 1);
                        distance_column = parseFloat(distance_column.toFixed(2));
                      }
                      break;
                    }
                  }
                }
              }
            };

            this.checkImagesLoad = function (more_selector) {
              more_selector = more_selector || '';
              var imagesSelector = '.ssw-instagram-media img' + more_selector;
              $container.find(imagesSelector).on('load', function () {
                ssw(this).next().hide();
                ssw(this).fadeIn("slow");
              });
              if (type === 'masonry') {
                $container.find('.ssw-instagram-media img').on('error', function () {
                  imageOnError(this);
                });
              } else {
                $container.find(imagesSelector).on('error', function () {
                  imageOnError(this);
                });
              }
            };
            var imageOnError = function (el) {
              var self = ssw(el);
              var replaced = self.attr('data-replaced');
              var objectLink = self.parent().data('object-link');
              var image_id = self.closest('.ssw-instagram-item').data('image-id');

              getImageByShortcode(objectLink).then(function (image) {
                self.parent().find('img').attr('src', image.low);
                self.parent().find('img').data('image', image.low);
                self.parent().find('img').closest('.ssw-instagram-link').data('image-url', image.standard);
                if (image.video_url)
                  self.parent().find('img').closest('.ssw-instagram-link').data('video-url', image.video_url);

                saveImage({
                  id: image_id,
                  low: image.low,
                  thumbnail: image.thumbnail,
                  standard: image.standard,
                  video_url: image.video_url
                }).then(function (response) {
                  if (typeof response.exists === 'undefined' && !response.exists) {
                    self.parent().closest('.ssw-instagram-item').remove();
                    if (type !== 'masonry') {
                      setItemsStyle();
                    }
                  }
                });
              },function () {
                var $item = self.closest(item_selector);
                if ($item.length) {
                  var id = $item.data('image-id');
                  ssw.ajax({
                    url: sswProxyUrl + '/lite2/service/index/removeImage',
                    type: 'post',
                    dataType: 'json',
                    data: {id: id, without_response: 0, gallery_id: '2'},
                    success: function (response) {
                    }
                  })
                }
                $item.remove();
                if (type !== 'masonry') {
                  setItemsStyle();
                }
              });
            };

            var getImageByShortcode = function (object_link) {
              return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {
                ssw.ajax({
                  url: object_link + '?__a=1',
                  type: 'GET',
                  success: function (response) {
                    var media = response.graphql.shortcode_media;
                    if (media.display_url) {
                      var thumbnail = media.display_url;
                    } else {
                      var thumbnail = media.display_resources[0].src;
                    }
                    resolve({
                      video_url: media.video_url,
                      low: media.display_resources[0].src,
                      standard: media.display_resources[1].src,
                      thumbnail: thumbnail,
                    });
                  }
                }).fail(function (xhr) {
                  if(xhr.status === 404)
                    reject()
                });
              });
            };

            var saveImage = function (data) {
              return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {
                ssw.ajax({
                  url: sswProxyUrl + '/lite2/service/index/saveimage',
                  type: 'POST',
                  data: data,
                  success: function (response) {
                    resolve(response);
                  },
                  error:function (err) {
                    reject(err);
                  }
                }).fail(reject);
              });
            };

            var setItemsStyle = function (more_selector) {
              more_selector = more_selector || '';
              $container.find(item_selector + more_selector).css('width', item_width + 'px');
              /*if (type == 'listing') {
                $container.find('.ssw-instagram-media').css('padding', distance_column + 'px');
              }*/
              $container.find(item_selector + more_selector).css('margin-right', distance_column + 'px');
              $container.find(item_selector + more_selector).css('margin-bottom', distance_column + 'px');

              $container.find(item_selector + ':nth-of-type(' + column_count + 'n)').css('margin-right', 0);
            };

            this.showItems = function () {
              if (type !== 'masonry')
                setItemsStyle(':hidden');
              $container.find(item_selector + ':hidden').fadeIn("slow");
            };

            this.initToScroll = function () {
              ssw(window).scroll(function () {
                if (!self.allowScroll) {
                  return;
                }
                if (self.scrollIsBottom()) {
                  scrolling = false;
                  if ($container.find(item_selector + ':hidden').length) {
                    if ($container.data('ajax-complete') && $container.data('page-count') > $container.data('page')) {
                      self.loadItems(false);
                    }
                    self.showItems();
                  }
                  else {
                    if ($container.data('ajax-complete') && $container.data('page-count') > $container.data('page')) {
                      self.showLoader();
                      self.loadItems(true);
                    }
                    else if ($container.data('page-count') == $container.data('page')) {
                      self.hideLoader();
                    }
                    else {
                      scrolling = true;
                      self.showLoader();
                    }
                  }
                }
              });
            };

            this.showLoader = function () {
              $container.find('.ssw-instagram-loader').show();
            };

            this.hideLoader = function () {
              $container.find('.ssw-instagram-loader').hide();
            };

            this.loadItems = function (show) {
              if ($container.data('ajax-complete') && $container.data('page-count') > $container.data('page')) {
                $container.data('ajax-complete', 0);
                var data_widget = $container.closest('.ssw-html-widget').data();
                var data = {
                  'module': 'service',
                  'name': 'InstagramPhotos',
                  'gallery_id': gallery_id,
                  'type': type,
                  'product_id': product_id,
                  '_sid': sswCookie('hesid'),
                  'hash_key': sswCookie('hash_key'),
                  'page': $container.data('page') + 1,
                  'rows_count': 1,
                  'columns_count_for_grid': 6,
                  'instagram_ipp': 15                };

                if(data_widget){
                  for(var key in data_widget)if(!data[key]){
                    data[key] = data_widget[key];
                  }
                }

                ssw.get(sswProxyUrl + '/lite2/core/widget/index', data, function (response) {
                  $container.find('.ssw-instagram-items').append(response);
                  $container.data('page', data.page);
                  if ($container.data('page-count') <= data.page) {
                    $container.find('.ssw-instagram-load-more').hide();
                  } else {
                                        $container.find('.ssw-instagram-load-more').show();
                                      }
                  self.checkImagesLoad(':hidden');
                  if (show || scrolling) {
                    self.hideLoader();
                    self.showItems();
                    self.loadItems(false);
                    scrolling = false;
                  }
                  $container.data('ajax-complete', 1);
                }, 'html', {
                  fail: function() {
                    self.hideLoader();
                  }
                });
              } else {
                self.hideLoader();
              }
            };

            this.scrollIsBottom = function () {
              if (ssw(window).scrollTop() >= $container.offset().top + $container.outerHeight() - window.innerHeight) {
                return true;
              }
              return false;
            };

            this.onContainerResize = function () {
              container_width = $container.width();
              self.initVars();
              if (type !== 'masonry')
                setItemsStyle();
            };

            ssw(window).resize(function () {
              if ($container.width() != container_width) {
                self.onContainerResize();
              }
            });

            this.initLoadMoreBtn = function () {
                            $container.find('.ssw-instagram-load-more').find('a').click(function (e) {
                if ($container.data('page-count') > $container.data('page') /*&& $container.data('page') <= 2*/) {
                  self.showLoader();
                  self.showItems();
                  self.loadItems(true);
                  $container.find('.ssw-instagram-load-more').hide();
                }
              });
            };
            self.checkImagesLoad(':hidden');
            self.initVars();
            self.showItems();
            self.initToScroll();
            self.initLoadMoreBtn();
            //self.loadItems(false);

            // Redraw items if container width changed
            if ($container.width() != container_width) {
              self.onContainerResize();
            }
          };

          var sswInstagramType = 'grid', sswInstagramGalleryId = 2, sswInstagramProductId = 0;

          var $sswInstagramContainer = ssw('#ssw-instagram-photos-' + sswInstagramType + sswInstagramGalleryId + sswInstagramProductId);

          var columnsCount = parseInt('6');
          var minColumnsCount = parseInt('2');
          var sswInstagramMinWidth = 250, sswInstagramMinDistance = 15;
          if (sswInstagramType === 'grid') {
            sswInstagramMinWidth = 220;
            sswInstagramMinDistance = parseInt('5');
          }
          new SswInstagramGallery($sswInstagramContainer, sswInstagramGalleryId, sswInstagramType, sswInstagramProductId, '.ssw-instagram-item', sswInstagramMinWidth, sswInstagramMinDistance, columnsCount, minColumnsCount);

          if (typeof customInstagramWidgetAfter == 'function') {
            customInstagramWidgetAfter();
          }

          ssw(document).ready(function () {
            if (navigator.userAgent.indexOf('Android') != -1 || navigator.userAgent.indexOf('android') != -1) {
              ssw('#ssw-instagram-android-upload').css('display', '');
            } else if (navigator.userAgent.indexOf('iPhone') != -1 || navigator.userAgent.indexOf('iphone') != -1 ||
              navigator.userAgent.indexOf('iPad') != -1 || navigator.userAgent.indexOf('ipad') != -1 ||
              navigator.userAgent.indexOf('iPod') != -1 || navigator.userAgent.indexOf('ipod') != -1) {
              ssw('#ssw-instagram-ios-upload').css('display', '');
            } else {

      event && event.hitCallback && event.hitCallback()
    }
  }
  
  Appmate.wk.on({
    'click .wk-variant-link': eventHandler(function(){
      var id = $(this).closest('[data-wk-item]').data('wk-item');
      var href = this.href;
      var product = Appmate.wk.getItem(id).then(function(product){
        var variant = product.selected_or_first_available_variant || {};
        sendTracking({
          hitType: 'event',
          eventCategory: 'Product',
          eventAction: 'click',
          eventLabel: 'Wishlist King',
          hitCallback: function() {
            document.location = href;
          }
        }, {
          id: variant.sku || variant.id,
          name: product.title,
          category: product.type,
          brand: product.vendor,
          variant: variant.title,
          price: variant.price,
        }, {
          list: "" || undefined
        });
      })
    }),
    'click [data-wk-add-product]': eventHandler(function(){
      var productId = this.getAttribute('data-wk-add-product');
      var variant = $(this).parents('form').find('select[name="id"]').val();
      Appmate.wk.addProduct(productId, variant).then(function(product) {
        var variant = product.selected_or_first_available_variant || {};
        sendTracking({
          hitType: 'event',
          eventCategory: 'Product',
          eventAction: 'add-to-wishlist',
          eventLabel: 'Wishlist King',
        }, {
          id: variant.sku || variant.id,
          name: product.title,
          category: product.type,
          brand: product.vendor,
          variant: variant.title,
          price: variant.price,
        }, {
          list: "" || undefined
        });
      });
    }),
    'click [data-wk-remove-product]': eventHandler(function(){
      var productId = this.getAttribute('data-wk-remove-product');
      Appmate.wk.removeProduct(productId).then(function(product) {
        var variant = product.selected_or_first_available_variant || {};
        sendTracking({
          hitType: 'event',
          eventCategory: 'Product',
          eventAction: 'remove-from-wishlist',
          eventLabel: 'Wishlist King',
        }, {
          id: variant.sku || variant.id,
          name: product.title,
          category: product.type,
          brand: product.vendor,
          variant: variant.title,
          price: variant.price,
        }, {
          list: "" || undefined
        });
      });
    }),
    'click [data-wk-remove-item]': eventHandler(function(){
      var item = this.getAttribute('data-wk-remove-item');
      var productId = this.getAttribute('data-wk-product-id');
      fadeOrNot($(this), function() {
        Appmate.wk.removeItem(item).then(function(product){
          var variant = product.selected_or_first_available_variant || {};
          sendTracking({
            hitType: 'event',
            eventCategory: 'Product',
            eventAction: 'remove-from-wishlist',
            eventLabel: 'Wishlist King',
          }, {
            id: variant.sku || variant.id,
            name: product.title,
            category: product.type,
            brand: product.vendor,
            variant: variant.title,
            price: variant.price,
          }, {
            list: "" || undefined
          });
        });
      });
    }),
    'click [data-wk-clear-wishlist]': eventHandler(function(){
      var wishlist = this.getAttribute('data-wk-clear-wishlist');
      Appmate.wk.clear(wishlist).then(function() {
        sendTracking({
          hitType: 'event',
          eventCategory: 'Wishlist',
          eventAction: 'clear-wishlist',
          eventLabel: 'Wishlist King',
        });
      });
    }),
    'click [data-wk-share]': eventHandler(function(){
      var params = $(this).data();
      var shareLink = Appmate.wk.getShareLink(params);
      
      sendTracking({
        hitType: 'event',
        eventCategory: 'Wishlist',
        eventAction: 'share-wishlist-' + params.wkShareService,
        eventLabel: 'Wishlist King',
      });
      
      switch (params.wkShareService) {
        case 'link':
          $('#wk-share-link-text').addClass('wk-visible').find('.wk-text').html(shareLink);
          var copyButton = $('#wk-share-link-text .wk-clipboard');
          var copyText = copyButton.html();
          var clipboard = Appmate.wk.clipboard(copyButton[0]);
          clipboard.on('success', function(e) {
            copyButton.html('copied');
            setTimeout(function(){
              copyButton.html(copyText);
            }, 2000);
          });
          break;
        case 'email':
          window.location.href = shareLink;
          break;
        default:
          window.open(
            shareLink,
            'wishlist_share',
            'height=590, width=770, toolbar=no, menubar=no, scrollbars=no, resizable=no, location=no, directories=no, status=no'
          );
      }
    }),
    'click [data-wk-move-to-cart]': eventHandler(function(){
      var $elem = $(this);
      $form = $elem.parents('form');
      $elem.prop("disabled", true);

      var item = this.getAttribute('data-wk-move-to-cart');
      var formData = $form.serialize();
      var productId = this.getAttribute('data-wk-product-id');
      
      fadeOrNot($elem, function(){
        Appmate.wk
        .moveToCart(item, formData)
        .then(function(product){
          $.ajax({
            url: '/cart.js',
            dataType: 'json',
            type: 'get',
            success: function (cart) {
              const freeProductTag = product.tags.find(function(tag) {
                return (tag.indexOf('free-') === 0)
              })
              
              if (freeProductTag) {
                console.log('trigger popup', freeProductTag)
                var linkId = 'upsell-popup-' + freeProductTag.replace('free-', '');
                console.log(linkId, $('a [href="#' + linkId + '"]'));
                $('a[href="#' + linkId + '"]').trigger('click');
              }
              $('.cart_count').text(cart.item_count);
            } 
          });
          $elem.prop("disabled", false);
          
          var variant = product.selected_or_first_available_variant || {};
          sendTracking({
            hitType: 'event',
            eventCategory: 'Product',
            eventAction: 'from-wishlist-to-cart',
            eventLabel: 'Wishlist King',
          }, {
            id: variant.sku || variant.id,
            name: product.title,
            category: product.type,
            brand: product.vendor,
            variant: variant.title,
            price: variant.price,
          }, {
            list: "" || undefined
          });
        });
      });
    }),
    'render .wk-option-select': function(elem){
      appmateOptionSelect(elem);
    },
    'login-required': function (intent) {
      var redirect = {
        path: window.location.pathname,
        intent: intent
      };
      window.location = '/account/login?wk-redirect=' + encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify(redirect));
    },
    'render .swiper-container': function (elem) {
      initSlider()
    },
    'ready': function () {
      $(document).ready(initSlider);
      $(document).ready(fillContactForm);
      $(document).ready(function(){
        if (typeof ga === 'function') {
    	  ga('require', 'ec');
  		}
      })
      
      var redirect = Appmate.wk.getQueryParam('wk-redirect');
      if (redirect) {
        Appmate.wk.addLoginRedirect($('#customer_login'), JSON.parse(redirect));
      }
      var intent = Appmate.wk.getQueryParam('wk-intent');
      if (intent) {
        intent = JSON.parse(intent);
        switch (intent.action) {
          case 'addProduct':
            Appmate.wk.addProduct(intent.product, intent.variant)
            break;
          default:
            console.warn('Wishlist King: Intent not implemented', intent)
        }
        if (window.history && window.history.pushState) {
          history.pushState(null, null, window.location.pathname);
        }
      }
    }
  });

  function initSlider () {
    if (typeof Swiper !== 'undefined') {
      var mySwiper = new Swiper ('.swiper-container', {
        direction: 'horizontal',
        loop: false,
        pagination: '.swiper-pagination',
        nextButton: '.swiper-button-next',
        prevButton: '.swiper-button-prev',
        spaceBetween: 20,
        slidesPerView: 4,
        breakpoints: {
          380: {
            slidesPerView: 1,
            spaceBetween: 0,
          },
          480: {
            slidesPerView: 2,
            spaceBetween: 20,
          }
        }
      })
    }
  }

  function fillContactForm() {
    if (document.getElementById('contact_form')) {
      var message = Appmate.wk.getQueryParam('message');
      var bodyElement = document.querySelector("#contact_form textarea[name='contact[body]']");
      if (bodyElement && message) {
        bodyElement.value = message;
      }
    }
  }

  function eventHandler (callback) {
    return function (event) {
      if (typeof callback === 'function') {
        callback.call(this, event)
      }
      if (event) {
        event.preventDefault();
        event.stopPropagation();
      }
    }
  }

  function fadeOrNot($elem, callback){
    if ($elem.parents('.wk-fadeout').length) {
      $elem.parents('.wk-fadeout').fadeOut(callback);
    } else {
      callback();
    }
  }
})();
function appmateSelectCallback(variant, selector) {
  var shop = Appmate.wk.globals.shop;
  var product = Appmate.wk.getProduct(variant.product_id);
  var itemId = $(selector.variantIdField).parents('[data-wk-item]').attr('data-wk-item');
  var container = $('#wk-item-' + itemId);

  if (variant) {
    Appmate.wk.updateItem(itemId, {selected_variant_id: variant.id});
  }
if(variant.inventory_management == null )
  {
    $('#made-to-order').val('This item is made-to-order.');
  }else if(variant.inventory_management == "shopify"  && variant.inventory_policy != "continue")
  {$('#made-to-order').val('');}
  var imageUrl = '';

  if (variant && variant.image) {
    imageUrl = Appmate.wk.filters.img_url(variant, 'large');
  } else if (product) {
    imageUrl = Appmate.wk.filters.img_url(product, 'large');
  }

  if (imageUrl) {
    container.find('.wk-variant-image').attr('src', imageUrl);
  }
  var formatMoney = Appmate.wk.filters.money;
  if (!product.hidden && variant && (variant.available || variant.inventory_policy === 'continue')) {
    container.find('.wk-add-to-cart').removeAttr('disabled').removeClass('disabled').attr('value', 'Add to Cart');
    if(variant.price <variant.compare_at_price){
      container.find('.wk-price-preview').html(formatMoney(variant.price) + " <del>" + formatMoney(variant.compare_at_price) + "</del>");
    } else {
      container.find('.wk-price-preview').html(formatMoney(variant.price));
    }
  } else {
    var message = variant && !product.hidden ? "Sold Out" : "Unavailable";
    container.find('.wk-add-to-cart').addClass('disabled').attr('disabled', 'disabled').attr('value', message);
    container.find('.wk-price-preview').text(message);
  }
  
  var totalPrice = 0;
  $('.wk-price > .money').each(function(index, elem) {
    var price = parseFloat($(elem).html().replace(/[^0-9\.-]+/g,""));
    if (price) {
      totalPrice += price;
    }
  });
  
  $('.wk-total-price').html(formatMoney(totalPrice.toFixed(2)));
};

function appmateOptionSelect(el){
  if (!Shopify || !Shopify.OptionSelectors) {
    throw new Error('Missing option_selection.js! Please check templates/page.wishlist.liquid');
  }

  var id = el.getAttribute('id');
  var itemId = $(el).parents('[data-wk-item]').attr('data-wk-item');
  var container = $('#wk-item-' + itemId);

  Appmate.wk.getItem(itemId).then(function(product){
    var selector = new Shopify.OptionSelectors(id, {
      product: product,
      onVariantSelected: appmateSelectCallback,
      enableHistoryState: false
    });

    if (product.selected_variant_id) {
      selector.selectVariant(product.selected_variant_id);
    }

    // Add label if only one product option and it isn't 'Title'.
    if (product.options.length == 1 && product.options[0] != 'Title') {
      $(container)
      .find('.selector-wrapper:eq(0)')
      .prepend('<label>' + product.options[0] + '</label>');
    }
  });
}
</script>
